# Appendix A. Data dictionary

> Generated by `scripts/manual/generate-appendices.ts` from the source tree. Do not edit by hand; rerun `bun scripts/manual/build.mjs`.

The schema declares **314 tables** across 38 schema files (`drizzle.config.ts` lists them). Each entry gives the table's purpose (from the comment above its definition), its owning domain and the files that write it (from `scripts/architecture-metrics.mjs`, the measure the one-writer ratchet uses), its columns and its indexes.

Column flags: **PK** primary key, **U** unique, **null** nullable. A foreign key is shown as `table.column` with its `ON DELETE` action. Allowed values are listed when the column declares a Drizzle `enum`. Dates are ISO 8601 text and money is integer cents unless a comment says otherwise.

## A.1 Identity, organizations and permissions

Users, organizations, memberships, teams, groups, invitations and permission overrides.

### account_deletion_requests

Account deletion is intentionally request-based: project and financial records may have contractual retention requirements, so an operator must review dependencies before removing the WorkOS identity and personal data.

- **Schema:** `src/db/schema.ts` (export `accountDeletionRequests`)
- **Owning domain:** `src/lib/domain/identity`
- **Writers (1):** `src/lib/domain/identity/store.ts`

### project_phase_proposals

One assembly of a contract estimate offered as a later project family phase. Its estimate (project_estimates.phase_proposal_id) is approved on its own. On acceptance the assembly leaves the contract estimate, or a deduct change order is drafted when that estimate is already signed, and the family phase is created, authorized at the accepted amount.

### trade_letters

A letter of intent or award letter sent to a selected trade for one estimate's accepted bid. The award letter is the written acceptance of the quote under the trade's master agreement; when the trade has no signed agreement yet it can ride along in the same envelope. Signed through Foxit.
